<Tabs sync={false}>
  <Tab title="Overview">

    Track Google Ads campaigns, ad groups, keywords, and conversions. Website: [ads.google.com](https://ads.google.com).

    **Connect**

    You connect Google Ads by signing in on the platform's website and letting Oviond read your data. Steps: [Connect a data source](/data-sources/connect).

    **Data views**

    A widget reads one data view: you pick it first, then its metrics and dimensions. Google Ads has 21 data views:

    | Data view | ID | Metrics | Dimensions |
    |---|---|---|---|
    | Account | `ACCOUNT` | 106 | 12 |
    | Campaigns | `CAMPAIGNS` | 106 | 23 |
    | Campaign Budget | `CAMPAIGN_BUDGET` | 99 | 15 |
    | Shopping Campaigns | `SHOPPING_CAMPAIGNS` | 100 | 32 |
    | Ad Groups | `AD_GROUPS` | 106 | 19 |
    | Ads | `ADS` | 106 | 26 |
    | Search Keywords | `SEARCH_KEYWORDS` | 103 | 26 |
    | Search Terms | `SEARCH_TERMS` | 99 | 11 |
    | Landing Pages | `LANDING_PAGES` | 99 | 11 |
    | Expanded Landing Pages | `EXPANDED_LANDING_PAGES` | 99 | 11 |
    | Video | `VIDEO` | 99 | 14 |
    | Placement | `PLACEMENT` | 99 | 13 |
    | Locations | `LOCATIONS` | 52 | 10 |
    | Geographic | `GEOGRAPHIC` | 99 | 21 |
    | Age | `AGE` | 99 | 12 |
    | Gender | `GENDER` | 99 | 12 |
    | Household Income | `HOUSEHOLD_INCOME` | 99 | 12 |
    | Parental Status | `PARENTAL_STATUS` | 99 | 12 |
    | Assets | `ASSETS` | 35 | 20 |
    | Extensions | `EXTENSIONS` | 35 | 15 |
    | Asset Groups | `ASSET_GROUPS` | 20 | 19 |

    Every metric and dimension, with its ID, format and summary, is in the **Metrics & dimensions** tab.

  </Tab>
  <Tab title="How it works">

    <Steps>
      <Step title="Connect Google Ads">
        In the client's **Data sources**, open **Google Ads** and sign in to your Google Ads account. Allow Oviond to read your data.
      </Step>
      <Step title="Pick the Google Ads account">
        Choose the Google Ads account.
      </Step>
      <Step title="Pick the ad account">
        Choose the ad account to report on. The list shows the ones inside the Google Ads account you picked.
      </Step>
      <Step title="Use it in widgets">
        Add a data widget, pick **Google Ads**, then a data view, metrics and dimensions. One connection can be linked to many clients.
      </Step>
    </Steps>

    **How the data arrives**

    Oviond asks Google Ads for the numbers when a widget is added or changed, when you click **Refresh**, and on the report's automatic refresh. Nothing is copied ahead of time. PDFs print the data saved in the report.

    **When the connection stops working**

    Oviond renews the Google Ads sign-in by itself. If the sign-in expires or access is removed, the connection shows **Needs attention**. Click **Refresh connection** and sign in again with the same account. Fixing it fixes every client that uses it. See [Test Connection](/data-sources/test).

  </Tab>
  <Tab title="Limitations">

    **Limits in Oviond**

    Each field's own rules are in the **Metrics & dimensions** tab.

    - **Connect it in the app.** AI tools (the MCP server and Ask Oviond) can't connect Google Ads, because it needs a sign-in in a browser. Once it is connected, they can link and use it.
    - **Some data views have advanced settings.** Under **Show Advanced Settings** in the widget's data settings you can change more options. Some settings take one choice, some take several.
    - **Deprecated fields are tagged.** Fields Google Ads no longer supports are marked **Deprecated** in the **Metrics & dimensions** tab and noted in the changelog. A widget that uses one may show an error or no data.

    <Limitations />

  </Tab>

Type","id":"click_type","description":"Splits the numbers by what people clicked, such as the headline, a call button or a sitelink."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Week","id":"week","description":"Splits the numbers by week, Monday to Sunday."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Day of Week","id":"day_of_week","description":"Splits the numbers by day of the week, such as Monday."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Conversion Action","id":"conversion_action_name","description":"Splits the tracked, such as a website, an app or a phone call."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Ad Group Name","id":"ad_group_name","description":"Splits the numbers by ad group."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Ad Group ID","id":"ad_group_id","description":"Splits the numbers by ad group ID, the unique number Google Ads gives each ad group."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Ad Group Type","id":"ad_group_type","description":"Splits the numbers by ad group type, such as standard Search or Display."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Ad Group Status","id":"ad_group_status","description":"Splits the numbers by ad group status, such as enabled, paused or removed."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Max CPA (1-Per-Click)","id":"ad_group_max_cpa","description":"Shows the target cost per conversion (target CPA) set on the ad group, in millionths of the currency (1,000,000 equals 1 unit)."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Max CPC (Micro)","id":"ad_group_max_cpc_micro","description":"Shows the highest amount the ad group bids for a click (max CPC), in millionths of the currency (1,000,000 equals 1 unit)."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Max CPM","id":"ad_group_max_cpm","description":"Shows the highest amount the ad group bids per 1,000 impressions (max CPM), in millionths of the currency (1,000,000 equals 1 unit)."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Display URL Domain","id":"auction_insight_domain","description":"Splits the Auction Insights numbers by the display URL domain of calendar year."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Device","id":"device","description":"Splits the numbers by device type, such as mobile, desktop or tablet."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Click Type","id":"click_type","description":"Splits the numbers by what people clicked, such as the headline, a call button or a sitelink."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Week","id":"week","description":"Splits the numbers by week, Monday to Sunday."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Day of Week","id":"day_of_week","description":"Splits the numbers by day of the week, such as Monday."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Conversion Action","id":"conversion_action_name","description":"Splits the numbers by conversion action, such as a purchase or a sign-up."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Conversion Category","id":"conversion_action_category","description":"Splits the numbers by the category of the conversion action, such as purchase or lead."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Conversion Adjustment","id":"conversion_adjustment","description":"Splits conversions into the original numbers and the later changes made by conversion adjustments."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"External Conversion Source","id":"external_conversion_source","description":"Splits the numbers by where the conversion was tracked, such as a website, an app or a phone call."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Ad Preview","id":"ad_preview","description":"Shows a preview of the ad, with its headlines, text and images."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Device Preference","id":"device_preference","description":"Splits the numbers by the device the ad prefers to show on, if one is set."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Display URL","id":"display_url","description":"Shows the web address shown on the ad."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Final App URLs","id":"final_app_urls","description":"Shows the app links the ad sends people to in mobile apps."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Final Mobile URLs","id":"final_mobile_urls","description":"Shows the landing page addresses the ad uses on mobile devices."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Final URL Suffix","id":"final_url_suffix","description":"Shows the text added to the end of the ad landing page URLs for tracking."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Final URLs","id":"final_urls","description":"Shows the landing page addresses people reach after clicking the ad."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Ad ID","id":"id","description":"Splits the numbers by ad ID, the unique number Google Ads gives each ad."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Ad Name","id":"name","description":"Splits the numbers by ad name."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Resource Name","id":"resource_name","description":"Shows the full Google Ads resource name of the ad, which includes the account and ad IDs."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Tracking URL Template","id":"tracking_url_template","description":"Shows the tracking template used to build the ad tracking URL."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Ad Type","id":"type","description":"Splits the numbers by ad type, such as responsive search ad or image ad."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"URL Collections","id":"url_collections","description":"Shows extra URLs saved on the ad, each with its own ID."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"URL Custom Parameters","id":"url_custom_parameters","description":"Shows the custom parameters added to the ad tracking URL."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Ad Status","id":"status","description":"Splits the numbers by ad status, such as enabled, paused or removed."}]},{"id":"SEARCH_KEYWORDS","label":"Search Week","id":"day_of_week","description":"Splits the numbers by day of the week, such as Monday."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Conversion Action","id":"conversion_action_name","description":"Splits the tracked, such as a website, an app or a phone call."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Keyword Text","id":"search_keywords_keyword_text","description":"Splits the numbers by keyword."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Match Type","id":"search_keywords_match_type","description":"Splits the numbers by keyword match type: broad, phrase or exact."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Keyword ID","id":"search_keywords_id","description":"Splits the numbers by keyword ID, the unique number Google Ads gives each keyword."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Keyword Status","id":"search_keywords_status","description":"Splits the numbers by keyword status, such as enabled, paused or removed."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Quality Score","id":"search_keywords_quality_score","description":"Splits the numbers by the keyword quality score, from 1 to 10."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Creative Quality Score","id":"search_keywords_creative_quality_score","description":"Splits the numbers by how well the ad text matches the keyword: below average, rating: below average, average or above average."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"Search Predicted CTR","id":"search_keywords_search_predicted_ctr","description":"Splits the numbers by how likely the ad is to get a click for the keyword: below average, average or above average."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"First Page CPC","id":"search_keywords_first_page_cpc_micros","description":"Shows the CPC","id":"search_keywords_top_of_page_cpc_micros","description":"Shows the estimated bid the keyword needs to show above the unpaid search results, in millionths of the currency (1,000,000 equals 1 unit)."},{"kind":"dimension","name":"First Position CPC","id":"search_keywords_first_position_cpc_micros","description":"Shows the estimated bid the keyword needs to show in the first position above
